# The Feature-To-Effect Matrix

Written by `node tools/effects.mjs --write` from `effects/*.json`; don't edit it by hand. One row a feature the code has, found
in the code (`tools/effects.mjs`, S1 of `criteria.md`), each read against it for the owner's brief 20: where the player
meets it, what it needs, what it reads, what it changes at once, which systems read that change later and where, when
and how the player meets the consequence, what a save keeps, the evidence and what's missing. The guide's promises, each
connection one entry makes to another, are counted apart. Only Implemented And Verified counts as done, out of every row
not Explicitly Deferred.

**Features:** 503 of 530 in scope Implemented And Verified (94.9%); 27 Implemented, Verification Incomplete, 0 Partially Implemented, 0 Not Implemented, 0 Blocked Externally, 0 Decision Required, 20 Explicitly Deferred. 415 mechanics, 135 presentation.

**The guide's promises:** 395 of 409 in scope Implemented And Verified (96.6%); 14 Implemented, Verification Incomplete, 0 Partially Implemented, 0 Not Implemented, 0 Blocked Externally, 0 Decision Required, 2 Explicitly Deferred.

### Benefits: The Guide's Promises

| Promise | What It Says | What Keeps It | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `promise.taxes.return>bank.accounts#immediate` | Filing takes any balance from your accounts at once, the checking account first and then savings. | tax.file pays each authority’s part with payFromBank, the checking account then savings (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:1649; src/engine/systems/statetax.ts:336). | `tests/b20-benefits-tax.test.ts`, `tests/b20-benefits-reach.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.taxes.return>day.needs` | Filing answers the year’s item in What Needs You. | tax.file answers the year’s decision (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:1623). | `tests/evidence-g33-ui.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.taxes.return>taxes.pay` | A balance your bank couldn’t pay at filing stays owed: the IRS’s bill comes at once, and its first reminder ${said(COLLECTION.noticeWeeks)} weeks after the deadline, or after filing if that’s later. | tax.file left owing sends the bill at once and the first reminder five weeks from the later of the due date and now (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:1657; src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:406). | `tests/remaining-tax.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.taxes.return>bank.accounts#scheduled` | A refund is paid into your checking account ${RETURN.refundDays} days after filing, or ${ITIN_REFUND_DAYS} days on a 1040-NR filed without a Social Security number. | The refund 21 days on, seven weeks more on a 1040-NR without a Social Security number (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:1642), paid to the payee account (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:1503). | `tests/b20-benefits-tax.test.ts`, `tests/defects-g8-8.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.taxes.return>papers.overview` | The head of Papers, and What Needs You on Home, say when an unfiled return is due, what filing it would pay (including any late penalty), and the tax you owe. | Papers’ head and What Needs You on Home read the open return’s due date, what filing would pay with its late penalty, and the tax owed (`papersAlerts`, src/ui/components/Glance.tsx:79). Home’s Papers line did until 2026-10-23 (brief 23). | `tests/evidence-g33-ui.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.taxes.return>documents.work` | On a 1040-NR, having a Social Security number before you file saves the ${said(SSN.itinWeeks)} weeks an ITIN application holds up the refund. | A 1040-NR filed without a Social Security number waits the ITIN’s weeks (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:1641). | `tests/b20-benefits-tax.test.ts`, `tests/defects-g8-8.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.taxes.pay>taxes.return` | You owe a balance only on a filed return, or on one the IRS or a state assessed without you. | A balance is set only by filing, an assessment or an amendment (setOwed’s callers); Pay refuses a year not owed (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:1671; src/engine/systems/statetax.ts:1007). | `tests/remaining-tax.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |

| `promise.taxes.pay>taxes.plan` | When you can’t pay at once, a plan stops collection for as long as you keep it up. | The IRS’s letters and levy wait while its plan stands (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:1455); a state’s while its own does (src/engine/systems/statetax.ts:969). | `tests/b20-benefits-tax.test.ts`, `tests/b20-benefits-reach.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |

| `promise.taxes.plan>taxes.pay` | A plan being decided or in force stops the letters and the levy on what you owe. | A plan decided or in force holds the letters and the levy, and its start cancels those pending (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:650). | `tests/b20-benefits-tax.test.ts`, `tests/remaining-tax.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |

| `promise.taxes.lien>taxes.pay` | A lien is released only once what it was filed for is paid in full. | A state’s release is asked only once paid (src/engine/systems/statetax.ts:740); the IRS’s releases itself only on payment in full (src/engine/systems/taxreturn.ts:315). | `tests/liens-and-arrangements.test.ts`, `tests/remaining-09-coverage.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |

### Life: The Guide's Promises

| Promise | What It Says | What Keeps It | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `promise.body.how>body.sleep` | A night is what moves energy, sleep debt and condition most. | applySleep moves energy, sleep debt and condition by the night’s hours and quality (src/engine/systems/life.ts:926, :929, :969); a waking hour moves them by a few points (life.ts:219). | `tests/b20-life-bite.test.ts`, `tests/wellbeing-bites.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.how>body.look-after` | A meal, a shower, a workout and an hour unwinding each move the measures they name, and the menu says by how much. | applyLookingAfter moves each action’s measures (src/engine/systems/life.ts:716); the menu’s figures are applyLookingAfter on a copy (lookAfterOptions, life.ts:797). | `tests/b20-life-bite.test.ts`, `tests/looking-after.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.how>coursework.class` | Readiness sets how much a class or a lab builds that day. | A class builds each skill by focusAt, read from readiness (src/engine/systems/school.ts:3516, :950). | `tests/evidence-g01-life.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.how>productions.nights` | A night on stage is judged with your readiness at curtain time: under ${EVALUATION.sufficientReadiness}, coming in tired costs you. | A night is judged with conditionAt’s readiness at the call (src/engine/systems/school.ts:967); under EVALUATION.sufficientReadiness the judgment names fatigue and takes it off (src/engine/systems/evaluation.ts:427). | `tests/integration-mastery.test.ts`, `tests/b20-life-fatigue.test.ts` | Implemented, Verification Incomplete: No test the gate runs plays a School night through the clock at readiness under 60 and reads its fatigue. The rule is the evaluation’s, held for a taped piece at readiness 30 (tests/integration-mastery.test.ts) and for a shoot day judged tired through the clock (tests/b20-life-fatigue.test.ts), which read the same conditionAt and sufficientReadiness. |
| `promise.body.how>school.sick` | Under ${ESTIMATE.sickBelow} condition, calling in sick is excused. | sickRefusal takes a sick day under ESTIMATE.sickBelow condition (src/engine/systems/school.ts:185), and the classes handler excuses the day (school.ts:3497). | `tests/whynot-school.test.ts`, `tests/evidence-g05-life.test.ts`, `tests/b20-life-bite.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.how>papers.conditions` | Long-term conditions are kept up or let go on Papers, and your own page says which. | health.treat keeps a long-term condition up or lets it go (src/engine/systems/health.ts:2387); the condition look says which (src/ui/looks/wellbeing.tsx, the condition look). | `tests/evidence-g04-life.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.sleep>body.how` | A night moves energy, stress, sleep debt, condition and morale by the time you wake. | applySleep at the wake: energy, stress, sleep debt, condition and morale (src/engine/systems/life.ts:926, :951, :929, :969, :977). | `tests/b20-life-bite.test.ts`, `tests/evidence-g08-life.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.sleep>day.calendar` | A commitment during the night goes ahead without you, and it counts as missed. | Each handler finds the sleeper absent: a plan (src/engine/world/plans.ts:138), classes (src/engine/systems/school.ts:1573), a work day (src/engine/systems/projobs.ts:1035). | `tests/evidence-g01-life.test.ts`, `tests/sleep-commitments.test.ts`, `tests/b20-life-bite.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.sleep>day.needs` | Commitments you slept through are named in a notice the next morning. | life.sleep names what it ran over in a notice as they wake (src/engine/systems/life.ts:439). | `tests/evidence-g01-life.test.ts`, `tests/b20-life-bite.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.sleep>school.standing` | Classes and calls you sleep through are absences the faculty weigh at the end of the term. | Classes slept through are missed, unexcused, on the term’s tally (src/engine/systems/school.ts:1573, :3533), which the term review reads against ESTIMATE.attendanceFloor (school.ts:3233). | `tests/b20-life-bite.test.ts`, `tests/evidence-g24-school.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.body.sleep>day.advance` | Skipping ahead through the night sleeps for you, but it’s a poorer night that stops at ${COASTING_ENERGY_CEILING} energy. | A jump of more than an hour through the night pays a night nobody chose at 0.95 of its quality, its energy held to COASTING_ENERGY_CEILING (src/engine/systems/life.ts:1337, :1352). | `tests/evidence-g08-life.test.ts`, `tests/b20-life-time.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |

| `tutorial.read` | command | The guide: I’ve Read It at the foot of the chapter that’s next (ui/guide/Guide.tsx, `markRead`), opened by Guide in the top bar, Read on the strip under it, or Read About It on a locked panel. | A life made with the guide (`universe.tutorial`), and the next chapter in order, each once. The chapters open in order.” The dialog draws I’ve Read It only on the next chapter (`isNext`); any chapter can be opened and read. | universe.tutorial.read and the chapters’ order (`CHAPTERS`, `nextChapter`). | Adds the chapter to universe.tutorial.read, and sets tutorial.finished once every chapter is read. Moves no clock and costs nothing; the guide holds the live clock while it’s open (`hold`). | isOpen: a part of the game is open once its chapter is read, or the guide is finished (`src/engine/world/tutorial.ts:51`); the engine’s guard: a command whose chapter isn’t read is refused, naming the chapter (lockedBecause) (`src/engine/world/tutorial.ts:100`); useFeature, behind every Gated button, locked panel and menu (`src/ui/guide/Guide.tsx:44`); the rail lists a place once its chapter is read (`src/ui/shell/GameShell.tsx:50`); an underlined word opens once its look’s chapter is read (`src/ui/looks/Look.tsx:87`); a screen opened by address is locked until its chapter is read (`src/ui/workspaces/WorkspaceRouter.tsx:34`); the strip under the bar and Guide (N Left) count what’s left (`src/ui/guide/Guide.tsx:140`) | At once: the dialog says Opened and lists what the chapter opened, with Try It and Next; Home’s panels fill in, the rail gains its places, the underlined words open and the strip moves to the next chapter. ### Guide: The Guide's Promises

| Promise | What It Says | What Keeps It | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `promise.start.home>start.guide` | A word becomes a link once you’ve read the guide chapter that covers it. | An underlined word is a link only once its look’s chapter is read (`guideOpen`, src/ui/looks/Look.tsx:87, by isOpen, src/engine/world/tutorial.ts:51); before, it’s drawn as plain words. | `tests/b20-guide-promises.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.start.home>day.weather` | The weather beside it shows the same town, hour by hour. | Home’s Today draws the town and the weather beside it from the one town (`place` and `weather`, both of `city`, src/ui/screens/Home.tsx:84 and :87); the weather’s look opens that town’s day hour by hour (src/ui/looks/weather.tsx:188). | `tests/b20-guide-promises.test.ts`, `tests/b20-guide-chapters.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.start.home>day.clock` | The local time is the game’s clock, so it moves when the clock does. | Local Time is the game’s clock in the town’s zone (`localTime: formatTime(sim.now, city.timeZone)`, src/engine/world/place.ts:156), in the town’s look; Today’s line says the same clock (src/ui/screens/Home.tsx:111). | `tests/b20-guide-promises.test.ts`, `tests/b20-guide-screens.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.start.guide>start.home` | Reading a chapter opens Home’s panels and the underlined words it names. | Each of Home’s panels is drawn once its chapter is read, and Locked before (`shows`, src/ui/screens/Home.tsx:65, `LockedPanel`, src/ui/guide/Guide.tsx:111), and its words open by the same rule (src/ui/looks/Look.tsx:87). | `tests/b20-guide-promises.test.ts`, `tests/b20-guide-chapters.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.start.guide>day.calendar` | “What’s Coming” opens the calendar, and the dialog that asks what to do when a commitment comes up. | “What’s Coming” opens ui.calendar and ui.go (src/engine/content/tutorial.ts:162): Up Next on Home (src/ui/screens/Home.tsx:362) and Go in the stop’s dialog (src/ui/shell/TimeControls.tsx:408), Locked before it. | `tests/b20-guide-promises.test.ts`, `tests/b20-guide-chapters.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.start.guide>day.clock` | With the guide, the clock starts paused, and reading “The Clock” opens Play. | A life made with the guide starts paused (src/engine/world/bootstrap.ts:359); Play is Locked and world.set_preferences refused until “The Clock”, which opens both (src/engine/content/tutorial.ts:175). | `tests/b20-guide-promises.test.ts`, `tests/tutorial.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.start.find>start.guide` | A place joins the menu once you’ve read its chapter. | The rail lists a place only once its chapter is read (`railGroups`, src/ui/shell/GameShell.tsx:50). | `tests/b20-guide-promises.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.start.find>day.needs` | Go There on a notice takes you to its screen, and to its tab where the notice names one. | Go There goes to the notice’s own route (src/ui/screens/Home.tsx:257, src/ui/screens/Updates.tsx:158), and the provider opens the screen at the tab a route names (`?tab=`, src/app/game-context.tsx:262), else where it was left. Until 2026-10-21 the promise said “its screen and tab”, though most notices name only their screen. | `tests/b20-guide-promises.test.ts`, `tests/b20-guide-screens.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.career.contracts>career.pay` | Each payment the contract promised is paid on its payday, and its state says where it stands. | Each promised payment is on the clock for its payday (`schedulePay`, src/engine/systems/propay.ts) and its state is read from the record and the clock (`payState`, src/engine/systems/contracts.ts:207). | `tests/career-contracts.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.career.contracts>career.offers` | Accepting an offer makes its contract. | pro.accept_offer writes the engagement's contract (`contractFor`, src/engine/systems/contracts.ts), as an option taken up does the next season's. | `tests/career-contracts.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
| `promise.career.contracts>career.unions` | A further episode or an option taken up past SAG-AFTRA’s thirty days needs you to be a member. | The season's refusal reads `sagWorkRefusal` for a further episode's days and an option's (src/engine/systems/projobs.ts, SEASON_HOOKS.refusal), so a non-member past the thirtieth day isn't called and the option lapses with why. | `tests/career-contracts.test.ts` | Implemented And Verified |
